# d8be509 Merge pull request #8 from steven91007/gitkb

## Summary
Merges PR #8 (branch gitkb) into master: the initial gitkb notes covering every commit after the history rewrite, the deploy workflow switch to fetch + reset --hard, and the .gitignore rules that keep certificates and gitkb scratch files out of version control.

## Why
Lands the generated knowledge base and the deploy fix needed after master's history was rewritten.

## Risks
Deploy now discards local changes on the VM checkout via reset --hard. The large batch of generated notes makes the merge diff noisy.

## Files
- `.github/workflows/deploy.yml` (M, +3/-1): Replaces `git pull` with `git fetch origin master` + `git reset --hard origin/master` in the SSH deploy script, plus an explanatory comment. [note](../changes/e30468c1bd7c73f8f8ee6ff0426be81b1b6df793ee2d4d52934c770c41f4cf6b.md)
- `.gitignore` (M, +3/-0): Appends an `ssl/*.pem` ignore rule with an explanatory comment. [note](../changes/44411fe181eea243d2d1935b859a164bfcfbfaa8fc674ee4f338c3bcb93216bb.md)
- `knowledge/changes/.gitkeep` (A, +0/-0): Empty placeholder that keeps the notes directory in version control. [note](../changes/acaffbd6933132aa030fd2b12b75136ae4429a4b98df9690cb60025c0be0ce37.md)
- `knowledge/changes/0003afc6224fc58b2aae0eb170bb4027ff67f084effafd266abf5cc5fb3178ff.md` (A, +38/-0): Generated gitkb change note for one file in one commit (front matter with path, status, line counts and change kind, then Summary and Notable symbols). [note](../changes/5d179e3a185541ac0c46a2cf767619e0ebf44fea7ec9cdf8963ebc89c7d3c20e.md)
- `knowledge/changes/0068caab4d66727310e20666b900878a6567133d91c80f701e95871e3b64e08d.md` (A, +34/-0): Generated gitkb change note for one file in one commit (front matter with path, status, line counts and change kind, then Summary and Notable symbols). [note](../changes/4a0b82d5d3fe97258baabcb50153aaae4afb7b28cfe15112097afd095b1a7dc4.md)
- `knowledge/changes/0095fb8a533a7d81562c2a770ff43ea976415656e21f8abef3927a1dddd6a1a7.md` (A, +34/-0): Generated gitkb change note for one file in one commit (front matter with path, status, line counts and change kind, then Summary and Notable symbols).
